# NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) Physics - Laws of Motion

## Topic 1: Newton's Laws of Motion

**Total Questions:** 110

---

### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Two blocks of mass 2. 9 kg and 1. 9 kg are suspended from a rigid support S by two inextensible wires each of length 1 m (see figure). The upper wire has negligible mass and the lower wire has a uniform mass of 0. 2 kg/m. The whole system of blocks, wires and support have an upward acceleration of 0. 2 m/s<sup>2</sup>. The acceleration due to gravity is 9. 8 m/s<sup>2</sup>.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0009105_CAD.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 110px; height: 177px;" />
Find the tension at the midpoint of the upper wire.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **50**

**Detailed Solution:**

To find tension at mid-point of the upper wire

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0009105_Ans..jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 203px; height: 218px;" />

The equation of motion gives

                  T<sub>2</sub> - 5g = 5a

Therefore  T<sub>2</sub> = 5(g + a) = 5(9. 8+0. 2) = 50 N

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The masses <math><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>, <math><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> are <math> <mrow> <mn>5</mn><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>kg</mtext></mrow> </math> respectively. These have been joined using massless, inextensible pieces of strings as shown in the figure. If the whole system is going upward with an acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mi> s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>, then the value of tensions <math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> respectively are [take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>]


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image16-b071d831-6827-45dd-ad65-0d722b7564f2.jpg" style="width: 88px; height: 242px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>29.4</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>N, 98 N, 98 N</mtext></mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>98</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>N, 49 N, 29.4 N</mtext></mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>118</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>N, 59 N, 35.4 N</mtext></mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>35.4</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>N, 118 N, 59 N</mtext></mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mtext>118</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>N, 59 N, 35.4 N</mtext></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given <math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9.8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>118</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>M</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9.8</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>59</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> 

<math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>(</mo><mn>9.8</mn></math>+2<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>35.4</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is released from height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi></math> on a smooth curved surface which ends into a vertical loop of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math>, as shown. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></math>, then

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image4-b7b1532a-5cbd-466e-ac6d-af905a74b5bd.jpg" style="height: 115px; width: 200px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** the particle reaches the top of the loop with zero velocity
- **(B)** the particle reaches the top of the loop with a non-zero velocity
- **(C)** the particle breaks off at a height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>r</mi></math> from base
- **(D)** the particle breaks off at a height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) the particle breaks off at a height <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>h</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>r</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

To reach the top of loop, particle must have a minimum speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math> at the bottom of the loop.

but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></math> , so the block won't reach the highest point of the track.

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The bob of the simple pendulum is given a vertically downward velocity of magnitude <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, as shown in the figure. The string can withstand a maximum tension equal to twice the weight of the bob and during motion the string breaks at an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>θ</mi></math> with the vertical. If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>m</mi><mi>n</mi></mfrac></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>n</mi></math> are smallest integers, then, the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi></math> is


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image33-6af253cd-e984-4fd4-904f-b108273cbf6f.jpg" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>
<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image34-a123c456-fd3d-4850-83b3-1ea8e04d6e0a.jpg" />

Let us assume that the length of the string is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi> </mi><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mo>…</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="bold-italic"> </mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> moves along the internal smooth surface of a vertical cylinder of the radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math>. Find the force with which the particle acts on the cylinder wall if at the initial moment of time its velocity equals <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>. and forms an angle <math><mrow><mi>α</mi> </mrow> </math> with the horizontal.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>sin</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mrow></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The direction of the velocity of the particle may be shown as in the diagram. This velocity V<sub>0</sub> is resolved in two components. Here <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>cos</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></math> component is tangential horizontal and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>V</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>sin</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></math> is along the surface but vertically downwards. Here, if the particle exerts a force N on the surface of the cylinder, the cylinder will also apply equal and opposite force on the particle


              <u><strong><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0027045_ANs_.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 160px; height: 200px;" /></strong></u>


and this will provide a centripetal force to the particle.

So,   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">N</mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">m</mtext><msup><mfenced><mrow><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">V</mtext><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>cos</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>/</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></math>

           <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>/</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></math>

or,   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext mathvariant="italic">N</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">mV</mtext><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mtext mathvariant="italic">R</mtext></mfrac><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#945;</mi></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The system shown in the figure is in equilibrium and all the blocks are at rest. Assume that the masses of the strings, the pulley and the spring are negligible with respect to the masses of the blocks and friction is absent. Find the acceleration of block <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>, just after cutting the string <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>. [<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>]

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ngpmmeyzvtvbjllthzjfwotoudqdvcplzrzvjkihneoiustlyl.jpeg" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Just after cutting the string 1 the extension of the string will remain same as it was before cutting the string '1'. So, we can take string force on block is equal '2g'.

Now making F.B.D of block 'B'.

<math><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>a</mi></math> .... (i)

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/hiamleiocsnsmvrabpvuaigkrfjvbtxeibeklxeskdwsuevcdx.jpeg" />

Making F.B.D of block 'c'

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/rqxbdwydmirugzqptibqcqcejopxmhppgoecqbbrvducyqoklw.jpeg" />

<math><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>a</mi></math> .... (ii)

From (i) and (ii)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

In the following arrangement, the system is initially at rest. The <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi><mo> </mo></math>block is now released. Assuming the pulleys and string to be massless and smooth. If the acceleration of block <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>10</mn></mfrac><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>, then find the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>. Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9.8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>.

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image29-72fa48c5-cbb5-436f-9438-c045efef259b.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 130px; height: 173px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **7**

**Detailed Solution:**

Block B will not move

<math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math> ...(i)

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> ...(ii)

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image30-17999e0d-727a-477c-b7ab-68d485245875.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9.8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10 </mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">m </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0.7</mn></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>7</mn></math>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A gun of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>kg</mi></math> fires <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math> bullets per second. The mass of each bullet is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> and the velocity of the bullet, when it leaves the gun, is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The force required to hold the gun while firing is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>24</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>240</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>24</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Rate of change of momentum of the bullet in forward direction = Force required to hold the gun.

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>300</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>24</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An elevator of mass <math> <mi>m</mi> </math> is connected to a rope which pulls the elevator up with a maximum acceleration equal to <math> <mi>a</mi> </math>. If the maximum bearable tension in the rope is <math> <mi>T</mi> </math>, then for a safe journey the minimum diameter of the rope is [<math> <mi>g</mi> </math> = acceleration due to gravity]

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The maximum tension in the rope  <math><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>)</mo></math> 

Stress in the rope  <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><msup><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A small ball of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mo>×</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> having a charge of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>μ</mi><mtext>C</mtext></math> is suspended by a string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. Another identical ball having the same charge is kept at the point of suspension. Determine the minimum horizontal velocity which should be imparted to the lower ball so that it can make a complete revolution.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 87px; top: -5.77778px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 173px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>11</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 65px; top: -5.77778px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>5.86 </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>5.86 </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If the ball has to just complete the circle then the tension must vanish at the topmost point, i.e.,T<sub>2</sub> = 0

From Newton&#39;s second law,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>T</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mtext>mg</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mn>i</mn></mfenced></math>

At the topmost point T<sub>2</sub> = 0,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>mg</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msup><mi>l</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mtext>ii</mtext></mfenced></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0028371_Ans.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 160px; height: 224px;" />

From energy conservation,

Energy at lowest point = Energy at topmost point

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>mg2</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>ii</mtext><mtext>i</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext>v</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mtext>u</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mtext>g</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>v</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>

From Eqn. (ii), <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mtext>v</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>m</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#160;&#8230;</mo><mfenced><mtext>v</mtext></mfenced></math>

From Eqns. (iv) and (v),

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>u</mtext><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mtext>g</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mtext>q</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mtext>m</mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>7</mn> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>/</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>86</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

In the arrangement shown in the figure, friction exists only between the two blocks, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>. The coefficient of static friction <math><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.6</mn></math> and coefficient of kinetic friction <math><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.4</mn></math>, the masses of the blocks <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>kg</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>kg</mi></math>, respectively. Find the acceleration (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>) of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math>, if a force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>150</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> is applied, as shown in the figure. [Assume that string and pulleys are massless]

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0261868_solu.JPG" style="width: 250px; height: 112px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let us assume that the two blocks move together, without slipping, relative to each other. The acceleration of the system in that case is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>F</mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>60</mn><mo>°</mo></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>150</mn><mo>×</mo><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mfrac bevelled="true"><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mstyle></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>30</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p>In this case, if the frictional force acting between the two blocks is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi></math>, then writing the Newton's second law of motion, for the block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1,</mn></msub></math> we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>a</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>150</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>120</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>f</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>200</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>120</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math></p><p>Since <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>≤</mo><msub><mi>f</mi><mi>max</mi></msub></math>, our assumption about the two blocks moving together is correct and hence the acceleration of the blocks is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0261868_solu.JPG" style="width: 250px; height: 112px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block of the mass of <math><mn>1 kg</mn></math> is moving on the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi></math>-axis. A force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math> acting on the block is shown. The velocity of the block at time <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. What is the speed of the block at time <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>?

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/nhmnhjogaxvwfxoynpvqljgivmoaytolxfbsivvemmouqonnux.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 180px; height: 144px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mtext>m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mtext>m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mtext>m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mtext>m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mtext>m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, the mass of block <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

The velocity of the block at time <math> <mrow> <mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>s</mtext></mrow> </math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Impulse during <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;s</mi></math> to <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#160;s</mi></math> .

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/yjrbhnyolxceflypttwxvuebrhxzowwazushdsiiascrtmgjle.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

From the graph,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#916;ABC</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#916;CDE</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mn>(</mn><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mn>)</mn><mn>(</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mn>)</mn></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

We know that,

Impulse = Change in momentum

<math><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mi>f</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>v</mi><mi>i</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>|</mo></math>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A hanging block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup></math> prevents the smaller block of mass <math> <mtext>m</mtext> </math> from slipping over a movable triangular block of mass <math> <mtext>M</mtext> </math>. All the surfaces are frictionless and the strings and the pulleys are light. Value of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup></math> in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/dsvavds.jpg" style="width: 230px; height: 156px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>cot</mtext> <mtext>θ</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow>  <mfenced open="[" close="]" separators=",">    <mrow>      <mfrac>        <mrow>          <mtext>m</mtext>          <mo>-</mo>          <mtext>M</mtext>        </mrow>        <mrow>          <mtext>cot</mtext>          <mtext>&theta;</mtext>          <mo>+</mo>          <mn>1</mn>        </mrow>      </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow>  <mfenced open="[" close="]" separators=",">    <mrow>      <mfrac>        <mrow>          <mtext>m</mtext>          <mo>-</mo>          <mtext>M</mtext>        </mrow>        <mrow>          <mtext>cot</mtext>          <mtext>&theta;</mtext>          <mo>-</mo>          <mn>2</mn>        </mrow>      </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow>  <mfenced open="[" close="]" separators=",">    <mrow>      <mfrac>        <mrow>          <mtext>m</mtext>          <mo>+</mo>          <mtext>M</mtext>        </mrow>        <mrow>          <mtext>cot</mtext>          <mtext>&theta;</mtext>          <mo>+</mo>          <mn>2</mn>        </mrow>      </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>cot</mtext> <mtext>θ</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Pulling force is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>, while mass in motion is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup></math>. So acceleration of the system will be given by
​<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>a</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mtext>m</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>g</mtext></mrow><mfenced><mrow><mtext>m</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>M</mtext><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>m</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>....(i)</mtext></math>

Mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> will not slide over <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> if 

          a cos&theta; = g sin&theta;                  ....(ii)

 From equation (i) in (ii), we get

<math> <mrow> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mtext>g cos &#952;</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>g sin</mtext> <mtext>&#952;</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>or</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <msup> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>cot &#952;</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A body of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> starts from the origin with an initial velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>30</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>˙</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>40</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>˙</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. If a constant force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="bold">i</mi><mo>˙</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mover><mover><mi mathvariant="bold">j</mi><mo>˙</mo></mover><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> acts on the body, the time (in seconds) in which the <math><mi>y</mi></math> component of the velocity becomes zero is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **40**

**Detailed Solution:**

From Newton's second law of motion,
 
<math><msub><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>△</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mo>△</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math><br. br="">

<math><mo>△</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>△</mo><msub><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>40</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>40</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math></br.>

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A ball of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> is thrown vertically upward by applying a force by hand. If the hand moves <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> while applying the force and the ball goes up to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> height further, find the magnitude of the force. Consider <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 78px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>16</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 78px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 78px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>22</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 78px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>22</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 78px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>W</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>T</mi><mi>E</mi></math>

<math><mi>F</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>h</mi></math>

<math><mi>F</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>22</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>200</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi></math> is being pulled by some men, up an inclined plane of inclination <math><mn>45</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math> and coefficient of friction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>, as shown in the figure. If each man can apply a maximum force of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>500</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>, then the minimum number of men required to just slide the block, up the plane is


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image5-844f2876-af25-4873-a127-75880623b1f4.jpg" style="width: 251px; height: 172px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>9</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Total force required


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image34-c11c60db-0801-4665-81ff-f7885cdd4473.jpg" style="width: 264px; height: 183px;" />

<math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>f</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></msub><mi>R</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>(</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></msub><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>200</mn><mo>×9.8</mo><mo>(</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>45</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.5</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>45</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>200</mn><mo>×9.8</mo><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

The number of men required will be

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>200</mn><mo>×9.8</mo><mo>×</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>(500)</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4.1</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A suitcase is gently dropped on a conveyor belt moving at a velocity of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. If the coefficient of friction between the belt and the suitcase is<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo> </mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>, the displacement of the suitcase relative to conveyor belt before the slipping between the two is stopped, is (<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 56px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>9</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 116px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>9</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Acceleration of the suitcase till the slipping continues is
<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mi>m</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#8203;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
Slipping will continue until its velocity also becomes <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>.
<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
or or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>
In this time, the displacement of the suitcase
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>s</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>a</mi><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0.6</mn></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0.</mn><mn>9</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
and the displacement of the belt,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>v</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0.6</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.8</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
Displacement of the suitcase with respect to the belt
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>s</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>s</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.9</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> stone tied at the end of a string <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> long is whirled along a vertical circle at a constant speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. What will be the tension in the string (in newton) at the bottom-most point?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **52**

**Detailed Solution:**

Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Tension at the bottom of the circle,

<math><msub><mrow><mi>T</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>L</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mn>0</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>4</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mn>1</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>52</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two blocks of masses <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> being connected with a light spring of stiffness <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> are driven with forces <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> on a smooth horizontal plane. If the blocks move with the same acceleration <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>, the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is:

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3f545e1a-4351-48f3-a30d-abc9bfdb0794.png" style="width:2.96667in;height:0.66667in" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** decided by the stiffness of the spring

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3f545e1a-4351-48f3-a30d-abc9bfdb0794.png" style="width:2.96667in;height:0.66667in" />

Both the blocks can be chosen as a system, thus,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two small balls of the same size, having masses <math><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> <math><mo>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>&#62;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>)</mo></math> are tied by a thin weightless thread and dropped from a certain height. Taking the force of buoyancy of air into account, the tension <math><mi>T</mi></math> in the thread during the flight, after the motion of the ball becomes uniform, will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mfrac><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mfrac><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mfrac><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Considering the equilibrium of lower ball,

<math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi> <mo>+</mo> <mi>F</mi> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mi>m</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>T</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mi>m</mi> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mi>m</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A stone of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> tied to a light inextensible string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mtext> m</mtext></math>  is whirling in a circular path of the radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi></math> in the vertical plane. If the ratio of the maximum tension to the minimum tension in the string is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn></math>. What is the speed (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) of stone at the highest point of the circle? (Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mn>0</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mtext>m s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> )

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **10**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0320804-2.jpg" style="width: 250px; height: 298px;" />

<math> <mrow> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>-</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>5 gL</mtext> </mrow> </math>

By energy conservation

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><msub><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>4 gL</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>3 gL</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mtext>10</mtext> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>v</mtext><mtext>A</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mtext>10 m&#160;s</mtext><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A nozzle throws a stream of gas against a wall with a velocity <math> <mtext>v</mtext> </math> much larger than the thermal agitation of the molecules. After collision of the molecules with wall  the magnitude of their velocity remains same. Also assume that the force exerted on the wall by the molecules is perpendicular to wall. (This is not strictly true for a rough wall.) Find the pressure exerted on the wall. <math><mo stretchy="false">(</mo> </math><math> <mrow> <mtext>n</mtext><mo>=</mo></mrow> </math> number of molecules per unit volume, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>=</mo></math>mass of a gas molecule<math> <mo stretchy="false">)</mo> </math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/lukkkaaaaa.JPG" style="width: 300px; height: 153px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><msup><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><msup><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><msup><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><msup><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mtext>sin</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><msup><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, the molecules rebound from the wall, the component of velocity perpendicular to wall is reversed,  while its velocity parallel to wall does not change. The change in velocity of molecules is parallel to normal N. The magnitude of charge is

                         <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>v</mtext> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>v</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

The change in momentum of a molecule is

                         <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mi>m</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mfenced close="|" open="|" separators=","> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <mover accent="true"> <mtext>v</mtext> <mo>→</mo> </mover> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

                          <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mi>m</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>v</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

in the direction of normal N. Let n be the number of molecules per unit volume.The number of molecules arriving at an area A of the wall per unit time is the number in a slanted cylinder whose length is equal to the velocity v and whose cross-section is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo> </mo><mi>cos</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>

       Number of molecules <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>Av</mi><mo> </mo><mi>cos</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>.

Each molecule suffers a change of momentum <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>mv</mi><mo> </mo><mi>cos</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>. Change of momentum of the stream of gas in a direction perpendicular to wall is equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mi>nAv</mi><mo> </mo><mi>cos</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>mv</mi><mo> </mo><mi>cosθ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi>An</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi>mv</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>.

Hence, force exerted by stream of gas on the wall.

                        <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext>A</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><msup><mtext>mv</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>

This is also the force exerted by gas molecules on the wall.

                        <math> <mrow> <mtext>Pressure</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>Normal force</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>Area</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

                            <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>⁡</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>⁡</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>

                            <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><msup><mo>⁡</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math>

</details>
