### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A stone hanging from a massless string of length <math><mn>15</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> is projected horizontally with speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The speed of the particle, at the point where the tension in the string is equal to the weight of the particle, is close to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>&#xA0;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#xA0;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>12</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>As, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math> we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>Also we know that</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>u</mi><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>12</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>93</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8776;</mo><mn>7</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The front wall of a drawer in a cabinet is a provider with two symmetrical handles. The distance between the handles is <math><mi>l</mi></math> and the length (i.e. depth) of the drawer is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi></math>. The maximum value of the coefficient of friction between drawer and cabinet, for which drawer can be pulled out by applying a force on one handle perpendicular to the face of the drawer is: (Neglect the weight of the drawer)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If a force is applied only on one handle, as shown in figure, the drawer will have a tendency to rotate clockwise. That is, the right and left corners (shown in figure), will press against the side of the cabinet and produce some friction.

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/64d87833-5bd2-4f75-87c5-2c82fdf96bd5-image3.png" style="height: 143px; width: 300px;" />

Balancing force,
<math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>μ</mi><mi>N</mi></math> …….(i)
Balancing torque (about right side corner):
<math><mi>F</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>N</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>μ</mi><mi>N</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>l</mi><mo>)</mo></math> ……..(ii)
Solving gives,
<math><mi>μ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block starts moving up a fixed inclined plane of inclination <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>60</mtext></mrow> <mo>&#8728;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math> with a velocity of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and stops after <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>2&#160;s</mtext></math>. The approximate value of the coefficient of friction is [<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>]

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math > <mtext>3</mtext></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>3</mtext><mtext>.3</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.27</mtext></mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.33</mtext></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.27</mtext></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Retardation of the block is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi><mfenced><mrow><mtext>sin</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>cos</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>u</mi><mi>t</mi></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><mfenced><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>20</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>27</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<div><p><span style="color:black">A smooth semicircular wire track of radius <math> <mi>R</mi> </math> is fixed in a vertical plane. <p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/e0912i4028hjqwepotuyowFK_JNKVMQ.PNG" style="width: 300px;" /></p><math> <mrow> <mtext>In <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo></mo><mo>∠</mo><mi>O</mi><mi>C</mi><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>60</mn><mo>°</mo> </mrow> </math> </span><p> </p><math> <mo>∴</mo> <mo>=</mo><mi></mi><mi>F</mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>+</mo><mi></mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow><mi></mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo> </mrow> </math></i></p><p><math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mi>F</mi> <mi>t</mi> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi> </mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>                               <math> <mo>∴</mo> </math>  <i>F<span style="position:relative;top:4.5pt">t</span></i> = <i>ma<span style="position:relative;top:4.5pt">t</span></i></p><p><math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn>5</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>m</mi><msub> <mi>a</mi> <mi>t</mi> </msub> </mrow> </math> <i><span style="position:relative;top:4.5pt">                             <math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mi>a</mi> <mi>t</mi> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>5</mn><msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mi>g</mi> </mrow> </math></span></i></p><p>Also when the ring is just released</p><p><i>N</i> + <i>F</i> sin30º = <i>mg</i> sin 30º</p><p><math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>–</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi> </mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi> </mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math></p><p> </p>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the system shown in figure. The wall is smooth, but the surfaces of blocks <math> <mtext>A</mtext> </math> and <math> <mtext>B</mtext> </math> in contact are rough. The friction on <math> <mtext>B</mtext> </math> due to <math> <mtext>A</mtext> </math> in equilibrium is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0027473_Question.jpg" style="width: 190px; height: 105px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** Upward
- **(B)** Downward
- **(C)** Zero
- **(D)** The system cannot remain in equilibrium

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) The system cannot remain in equilibrium**

**Detailed Solution:**

Considering <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mtext>A</mtext> </math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mtext>B</mtext> </math> as a system. There is no vertical force in upward direction to support their weight. Therefore, the system cannot remain in equilibrium.

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block of mass <math><mi>m</mi></math> is resting on a smooth horizontal surface. One end of a uniform rope of mass <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mi>m</mi> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> is fixed to the block, which is pulled in the horizontal direction by applying force <math><mi>F</mi></math> at the other end. The tension in the middle of the rope is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>F</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>F</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>F</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Acceleration of the system 

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Tension in the middle of the rope 

<math><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>a</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&sdot;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>F</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The minimum acceleration with which a fireman can slide down a rope, if the breaking strength of the rope is <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> of his weight, is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(D)** zero

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>g</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If the fireman slides down with some acceleration, his apparent weight decreases. For critical condition, rope can bear only <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> of his weight. If <math><mi>a</mi></math> is the minimum acceleration then,

Tension in the rope = Breaking stress <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>g</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A <math><mn>2000</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> rocket in free space expels <math><mn>0.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> of gas per second at exhaust velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>400</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>. The increase in the speed of the rocket in this time is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2000</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** zero

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>0.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>M</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>400</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2000</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>v</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>m&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A wedge of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>, lying on a rough horizontal plane, is acted upon by a horizontal force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and another force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>, inclined at an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> to the vertical. The block is in equilibrium, then the minimum coefficient of friction between it and the surface is
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/465095.JPG" style="width: 280px; height: 221px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>sin&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>cos&#952;</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>cos&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>mg</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>sin&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>sinθ</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>cosθ</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mi>sinθ</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>cosθ</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>sinθ</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>cosθ</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/465095-1.JPG" style="width: 260px; height: 223px;" /></p><p>We know <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>μN</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>cosθ</mi></math></p><p>Now, since wedge is in equilibrium,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>sinθ</mi><mo>)</mo><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi>cosθ</mi><mo>)</mo></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> sits on the top of a block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi></math> which sits on a table. The coefficient of kinetic friction between all surface is <math><mi>μ</mi><mi> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mn>1</mn></math> . A massless string is connected to each mass and wraps halfway around a massless pulley, as shown. Assume that you pull on the pulley with a force of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math>. The acceleration of your hand is -

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/nnldxaexawppspotykwwbuushrxjqtukffudmxerqilrlfijgc.jpeg" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>g</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The free body diagrams are :

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/gwfifwfbjfougitgvebvvufogtssudkvnjuunahjmuhbethznn.jpeg" />

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is force of friction between blocks

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is force of friction between block and ground

Making FBD of pulley

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/qouawkolhdgdiringvlqpbyxbdeztinnudpabzoqmrretqpbiq.jpeg" />

Since pulley is light

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>mg</mi></math>

Let blocks move together with common acceleration a

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi></math>

then for upper block

<math><mi>T</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math>

but <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> should be less than <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>f</mi><mi>max</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math></p><p>So, our assumption of no relative motion b/w blocks is incorrect that means there is relative motion

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> is kinetic <math><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

acceleration of pulley = accelerated of hand

<math><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<p>The figure below shows a block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math>, kept on a rough inclined plane of angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo></math> and coefficient of static friction equal to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>. It is being acted upon by two forces. What should be the minimum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>) so that the block doesn't slip downwards? <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo></math>Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi>ms</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>7</mn></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>]</mo></math></p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0627072.PNG" style="width: 230px; height: 122px;" /></p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **16.25**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>mg</mtext><mi>sin</mi><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mo>+</mo><mi>μ</mi><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mg</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>cos</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>15</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mn>80</mn><mo>=</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mo>+</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>75</mn><msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>80</mn><mo>−</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>75</mn><msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>160</mn><mo>−</mo><mn>75</mn><msqrt> <mn>3</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>16.25</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>N</mtext></mrow> </math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/image3241.JPG" style="width: 254px; height: 193px;" />

</details>

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A small spherical ball (obeying Stoke's law for viscous force) is thrown up vertically with a speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and is received back by the thrower at the point of projection with a speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. Neglecting the buoyant force on the ball, assuming the speed of the ball during its flight to be never equal to its terminal speed and taking the acceleration due to gravity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>, find the time of flight of the ball in seconds.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">m</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">d</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">d</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#8201;</mtext><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="italic">k</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">m</mi></mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#8201;</mtext><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#8201;</mtext><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mtext mathvariant="italic">&#8201;</mtext><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p>Integrating both sides</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="italic">k</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">m</mi></mfrac><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></msubsup><mi>d</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p>The total displacement of the ball in time <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>T</mi></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mn>0</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></msubsup><mover><mi>v</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>T</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>v</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">u</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>u</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>u</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>v</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>u</mi></mrow><mi>g</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

As shown in the following figures, block of mass <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math> is in equilibrium in the vertical plane.


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image51-e9ed86d4-3d80-427f-9915-375527cd65f9.jpg" />

At a certain instant right spring in case-<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> and right string in case-<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> are cut. Then the ratio of instantaneous acceleration of block in case-<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> to case-<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> just after the cutting is (strings and springs are ideal)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** none of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image52-3631e278-be35-4326-a50a-d54a0d01e22c.jpg" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>Tsin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>37</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>50</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

Now when spring is cut in case-<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn></math> then net force <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math> acting on block is,

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>50</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>50</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>                    (resultant force of the remaining <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>50</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>mg</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> forces)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>50</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

Similarly, when the string is cut in case-<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math> then net force <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> acting on block is <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>50</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>50</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>48</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>24</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5000</mn><mo> </mo><mo>kg</mo></math> rocket is set for vertical firing. The exhaust speed is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>800</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. To give an initial upward acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo> m</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> the amount of gas ejected per second to supply the needed thrust (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kg</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) will be (Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> m</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **187.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<b>Given :</b> Mass of rocket <math><mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>5000</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Kg</mi></mrow></math>

Exhaust speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>800</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Acceleration of rocket <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Gravitational acceleration <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

We know that upward force

<math><mrow><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>5000</mn><mrow><mo>(</mo><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>20</mn></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>5000</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>150000</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>N</mn></mrow></math>

We also know that amount of gas ejected

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>F</mi><mi>v</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>150000</mn><mn>800</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>187.5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A small ring of mass m is constrained to slide along a horizontal wire fixed between two rigid supports. The ring is connected to a particle of same mass by an ideal string &amp; the whole system is released from rest as shown in the figure. If the coefficient of friction between ring A and wire is <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></math>, the ring will start sliding when the connecting string will make an angle <math> <mrow> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math> with the vertical, then <math> <mrow> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math> will be (particle is free to move and ring can slide only)


          <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0067036_solution.png" style="width: 220px; height: 62px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** 30<sup>o</sup>
- **(B)** 45<sup>o</sup>
- **(C)** 60<sup>o</sup>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 45<sup>o</sup>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0067036_Question.png" style="width: 252px; height: 120px;" /></p><p>Using energy conservation, we get</p><p><math> <mrow> <mo>mgl cos</mo> <mi>&#952;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>mv</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>mgcos&#952;</mi></math></p><p>As the mass is moving in a circular motion, applying circular motion equation, 

<math> <mrow> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>mg cos</mtext> <mi>&#952;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>mg cos</mtext> <mi>&#952;</mi> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mtext>mg cos</mtext> <mi>&#952;</mi> </mrow> </math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Tsin&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mi>mg</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>Tcos&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mtext>mg cos</mtext> <mi>&#952;</mi> <mo>sin</mo> <mi>&#952;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mtext>mg</mtext> <msup> <mo>cos</mo> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>&#952;</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>&#952;</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#8728;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math></p>

</details>

---

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A monkey of mass 20 kg is holding a vertical rope. The rope will not break when a mass of 25 kg is suspended from it, but will break if the mass exceeds 25 kg. What is the maximum acceleration with which the monkey can climb up along the rope? <math> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0 </mn> <msup> <mtext>m s</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.5</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8203;&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>5</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><mtext>​ </mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>10 </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><mtext>​ </mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>25 </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><mtext>​ </mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.5</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>&#8203;&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For monkey, we can write

Mg - T = Ma

or M(g + a) = T              ....(i)

for suspended weight,

T ≤ mg                 ....(ii)

T ≤ 25 × 10 or 250 N [breaking strength]

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/IL0001898_AnsExp_1.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 150px; height: 238px;" />​

∴    Equation (i) becomes
  
   20.(10 + a) ≤ 250

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>≤</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>50</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>or</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>≤</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>5</mn> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <msup> <mtext>m s</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

∴ a<sub>max</sub> = 2.5 m s<sup>-2</sup>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A car is moving in a circular horizontal track of radius <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mtext>m</mtext></math> A plumb bob is suspended from the roof of the car by a light rigid rod. The angle made by the rod with the vertical is <math><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo></math> Then the car moves with a constant speed of _______ <math><mtext>m</mtext><mo>/</mo><mtext>s</mtext><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>g</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>10 </mn><mi></mi><mtext>m</mtext><mo>/</mo><msup><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **6**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/0e6db851-39b5-448b-ba22-db220d12e8c3.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.79375in;height:1.92014in" />
<math><mtext>T</mtext><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext>mv</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mtext>R</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mtext>T</mtext><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mtext>mg</mtext></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mtext>v</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mtext>Rg</mtext></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>×</mo><mi>R</mi><mo>×</mo><mi>g</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>36</mn></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6 </mn><mi></mi><mtext>m</mtext><mo>/</mo><mtext>s</mtext></math>

</details>

---

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Three blocks <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> each of mass <math><mn>2</mn><mi> </mi><mn>k</mn><mn>g</mn><mo>,</mo></math> are hanging over a fixed pulley as shown in the figure. The tension in the string connecting <math><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> is

<img height="201" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/izmcxhieqwilnbmrmjhgpcgmlndyzeltjlfgvlksiwuhzlsfkq.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" width="110" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** Zero
- **(B)** <math><mn>3.3</mn><mi> </mi><mn>N</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>13.3</mn><mi> </mi><mn>N</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>19.6</mn><mi> </mi><mn>N</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>13.3</mn><mi> </mi><mn>N</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The combination of blocks <math><mi>A</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><mi>B</mi></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> is shown in figure.

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/hkxuqkkrpmvopztudclysqbiscghqeplhkoofgkkhdyxrtyvfj.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/wjzkcbngltkozsaeqlsfjwjnhcmwthwzfctqujilkumnpgmffg.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

<math><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>13.3</mn><mi> </mi><mn>N</mn></math>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The figure below shows the top view of a hollow cylindrical room having a frictionless floor. A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is projected with a speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi></math>, on the smooth horizontal floor, along the wall of the room. The average normal force exerted by the cylindrical walls, on the block, during its motion from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3eeede.JPG" style="width: 120px; height: 154px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The change in momentum of the block from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is</p><p><math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>v</mi></math></p><p>The time it takes to move from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow><mi>v</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>The average force exerted by the wall on the block is</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mi>avg</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>R</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A small block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> slides along a smooth frictional track as shown in the figure. If it starts from rest at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>, what is the resultant force acting on it at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></math>?

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/image__6_.png" style="width: 300px; height: 173px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>70</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">mg</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>60</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">mg</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>75</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">mg</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><msqrt><mn>65</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">mg</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msqrt><mn>65</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">mg</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/dddere_phys.png" style="width: 250px; height: 124px;" />

From energy conservation,

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msqrt></math>

At <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

So net force at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>65 </mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A pendulum has an angular amplitude <math><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></math>. The tension in the string at extreme position is T<sub>1</sub> and at the bottom is T<sub>2</sub>. If it is known that T<sub>2</sub> = 2T<sub>1</sub>, then what is the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cos</mi><mi>θ</mi></math>?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.75**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>mg cos</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>       ..... (1)

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>gh</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

             <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>mg</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext> </mrow> </math>

             <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext mathvariant="italic">l</mtext> </mrow> </math>

or   <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>    ....... (2)

<u><strong><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0021690_AnsExp_1.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 273px; height: 193px;" /></strong></u>

Given T<sub>2</sub> = 2T<sub>1</sub>

or  <math> <mrow> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>mg</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>2mg cos</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

or  <math> <mrow> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mi>θ</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
                = 0.75

</details>

---

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>A particle of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is fastened to one end of a string and another particle of mass <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> is attached to the middle point, the other end of the string being fastened to a fixed point on a smooth horizontal table. If the particles are made to revolve in a horizontal circular path as shown in the figure, then the ratio of tension in the part of the string between the centre and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> to the tension in the part of the string between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image63-33074a96-2d43-45a9-8ef2-542fa7377990.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 148px; width: 150px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>
<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image64-b75188f4-e5a0-4f05-a69e-cf3a219233eb.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />



for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>,</mo></math></p><p>        <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> ......(i)

for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo></math></p><p>       <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math> ....(ii)</p><p>Dividing both the equations:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></math></p>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A person holding a bag starts sliding freely from a point A on a frictionless inclined plane while his bag starts falling down vertically from the same point. The final speeds of the man <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and the bag <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> on reaching the ground after falling through the same height should be such that

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** They depend on the masses
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

As there is only a gravitational field which is a conservative field, the change in kinetic energy of both the objects will be proportional to their masses and their vertical displacements. Hence, their speeds will be only proportional to their vertical displacements.

<math><mi>s</mi><mi>o</mi><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>M</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

---

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A conveyor belt is moving at a constant speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. A box is gently dropped on it. The coefficient of friction between them is <math><mi>μ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.5</mn><mo>.</mo></math> The distance that the box will move relative to the belt before coming to rest on it taking <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 83px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 83px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(C)** Zero
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 83px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 83px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Frictional force on the box <math><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math> Acceleration in the box

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi>u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mi>s</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>)</mo><mi>s</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> (with respect to the belt)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi>distance</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.4</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5000</mn><mo> </mo><mo>kg</mo></math> rocket is set for vertical firing. The exhaust speed is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>800</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. To give an initial upward acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo> m</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></math> what is the amount of gas ejected (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>kg</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) to supply the needed thrust? (Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> m</mo><mo> </mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **187.5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<b>Given :</b> Mass of rocket <math><mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>5000</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Kg</mi></mrow></math>

Exhaust speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>800</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Acceleration of rocket <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>a</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Gravitational acceleration <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mn>s</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

We know that upward force

<math><mrow><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>a</mi></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>5000</mn><mrow><mo>(</mo><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>20</mn></mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>5000</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>150000</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>N</mn></mrow></math>

We also know that amount of gas ejected

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mrow><mo>(</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>F</mi><mi>v</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>150000</mn><mn>800</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>187.5</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>
