### Q93. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is placed at the lowest point of a smooth vertical track of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>R</mi></math>. In this position, the block is given a horizontal velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi></math> such that the block is just able to perform a complete vertical circular motion. The acceleration of block, when its velocity is vertical is</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/bbbbb.JPG" style="width: 200px; height: 187px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>3g</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mi>g</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The initial velocity of the block to undergo a complete vertical circular motion is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></msqrt></math></p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/bbbbb-11.JPG" style="width: 165px; height: 250px;" /></p><p>Let us assume that the speed of the block when it is moving in the vertical direction is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>v</mi></math>, then using conservation of mechanical energy we get</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mfenced><msqrt><mn>5</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></msqrt></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>1</mn></mstyle><mstyle displaystyle="true"><mn>2</mn></mstyle></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>R</mi></msqrt></math></p><p>The tangential and centripetal accelerations of the block are</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mo>&#8595;</mo></mfenced></math> and  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>g</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#160;</mo><mfenced><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8592;</mo></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>a</mi><mi>net</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>10</mn></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q94. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A small sphere of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> suspended by a thread is first taken aside so that the thread forms a right angle with the vertical and then released. The total acceleration of the sphere when it makes an angle <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#952;</mi></math> with the vertical is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mtext>cos</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#x3B8;</mi></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Between A and B, <math> <mrow> <mtext> mgL  cos </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msubsup> <mtext>mv</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴    </mo> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>gL  cos </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

Now, <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>r</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>v</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>L</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>g  cos  </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>                

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0014783_AnsExp_1_1.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 126px; height: 128px;" />

and <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>t</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>g </mtext> <mtext>sin </mtext> <mi>θ</mi> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴    </mo> <mtext>a</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msqrt> <msubsup> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>t</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>+</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>a</mtext> <mtext>r</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </msqrt> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>g</mtext> <msqrt> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mn>3</mn> <msup> <mtext>cos</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>θ</mi> </msqrt></mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q95. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What is the maximum value of the force <math><mtext>F</mtext></math> such that the block shown in the arrangement, does not move?

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/IL0000447_questi_1.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 220px; height: 91px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>20</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<div><p style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-autospace:none"><span style="color:black">The forces acting on the block are shown. Since the block is not </span></p><p style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-autospace:none"><span style="color:black">moving forward for the maximum force </span><i><span>F</span></i><span> applied, therefore </span></p><p style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-autospace:none"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>N</mi> </mrow> </math> </span><span>    &hellip;(i) (Horizontal Direction)</span></p><p style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-autospace:none"><span>and  <math> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mi>sin</mi><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>N</mi> </mrow> </math>    &hellip;(ii)</span></p><p align="left" style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-align:left;"><span>From (i) and (ii)</span></p><p align="left" style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-align:left;"><span><img alt="" height="215" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/IL0000447_sol_4_1.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" width="327" /></span></p><p align="left" style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mi>F</mi><mi>sin</mi><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi> </mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow> </mrow> </math> </span></p><p align="left" style="margin-right:-.9pt;text-align:left;"><span style="position:relative;top:0.0pt"> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>sin</mi><mn>60</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#8722;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> </span></p></div>

</details>

### Q96. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A hanging block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup></math> prevents the smaller block of mass <math><mtext>m</mtext> </math> from slipping over a movable triangular block of mass <math> <mtext>M</mtext></math>. All the surfaces are frictionless and the strings and the pulleys are light. Value of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup></math> in terms of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/dsvavds.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 200px; height: 136px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>cot</mtext> <mtext>θ</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow>  <mfenced open="[" close="]" separators=",">    <mrow>      <mfrac>        <mrow>          <mtext>m</mtext>          <mo>-</mo>          <mtext>M</mtext>        </mrow>        <mrow>          <mtext>cot</mtext>          <mtext>&theta;</mtext>          <mo>+</mo>          <mn>1</mn>        </mrow>      </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow>  <mfenced open="[" close="]" separators=",">    <mrow>      <mfrac>        <mrow>          <mtext>m</mtext>          <mo>-</mo>          <mtext>M</mtext>        </mrow>        <mrow>          <mtext>cot</mtext>          <mtext>&theta;</mtext>          <mo>-</mo>          <mn>2</mn>        </mrow>      </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow>  <mfenced open="[" close="]" separators=",">    <mrow>      <mfrac>        <mrow>          <mtext>m</mtext>          <mo>+</mo>          <mtext>M</mtext>        </mrow>        <mrow>          <mtext>cot</mtext>          <mtext>&theta;</mtext>          <mo>+</mo>          <mn>2</mn>        </mrow>      </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>cot</mtext> <mtext>θ</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Pulling force is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>, while mass in motion is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>'</mo></msup></math>. So acceleration of the system will be given by
​<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>a</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mtext>m</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup><mtext>g</mtext></mrow><mfenced><mrow><mtext>m</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>M</mtext><mo>+</mo><msup><mtext>m</mtext><mo>'</mo></msup></mrow></mfenced></mfrac><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mspace width="0.5em"/><mtext>....(i)</mtext></math>

Mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> will not slide over <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> if 

          a cos&theta; = g sin&theta;                  ....(ii)

 From equation (i) in (ii), we get

<math> <mrow> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mtext>g cos &#952;</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>g sin</mtext> <mtext>&#952;</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>or</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <msup> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>&#8242;</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>m</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>M</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>cot &#952;</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q97. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle is given a horizontal velocity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi></math>, from the point <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> on a smooth horizontal floor which has a vertical circular track at the end. <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is a semicircular track of radius <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>r</mi></math> in the vertical plane. If the path length <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>r</mi></math> and the particle returns to point <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>, then the initial speed of the particle is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image12-2907c7c9-57d2-4169-b6f5-c7016ce93daa.jpg" style="height: 147px; width: 180px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><mi>g</mi><mi>r</mi></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>…</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>


<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image13-55689c2e-7bba-4a38-912a-3bcded168ec6.jpg" style="height: 169px; width: 200px;" />

or, <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

as, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>9</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

From equation (1) <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></math> or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></msqrt></math>

</details>

### Q98. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The minimum force required to move a body up an inclined plane is three times the minimum force required to prevent it from sliding down the plane. If the coefficient of friction between the body and the inclined plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the angle of the inclined plane is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>60</mn><mo>°</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>45</mn><mo>°</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>15</mn><mo>°</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Minimum force required to move a body up a rough inclined plane,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></math>

Minimum force required to prevent the body from sliding down the rough inclined plane,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>mg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

According to the question,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>mg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>mgsin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;mgcos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math>

</details>

---

### Q99. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The figure shows the position-time <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> graph of one-dimensional motion of a body of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math>. The magnitude of each impulse is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image1-e7cfc87c-e1ed-468e-8ad6-d2d217d6e5fd.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 300px; height: 143px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 57px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 57px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 57px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 57px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 57px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

From the graph, it is a straight line so, uniform motion. Because of impulse direction of velocity changes as can be seen from the slope of the graph.

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="bold">J</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>0.4</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">J</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>p</mi><mi>u</mi><mi>l</mi><mi>s</mi><mi>e</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="bold-italic">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">J</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math>

</details>

### Q100. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A particle is suspended by a light vertical inelastic string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>l</mi></math> from a fixed support. At its equilibrium position, it is projected horizontally with a speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mn>6</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi></msqrt></math>. What is the ratio of the tension in the string in its horizontal position to when the particle is vertically above the point of support?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

For a given <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>u</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>6</mn><mi>g</mi><mi>l</mi></msqrt></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0023824_AnsExp_2.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 326px; height: 155px;" />
At the horizontal position,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mtext>4 </mtext><mtext mathvariant="italic">mg</mtext></math>                .......... (i)

At the highest point

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mn>2</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup></mrow><mi>l</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mtext>2 </mtext><msub><mtext mathvariant="italic">mgT</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mtext mathvariant="italic">mg</mtext></math>             ......... (ii)

From the above equations, we get

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi>T</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q101. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

A body of weight <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>64</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math> is pushed with just enough force to start it moving across a horizontal floor and the same force continues to act afterwards. If the coefficients of static and dynamic friction are <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>4</mn></math> respectively, the acceleration of the body will be (Acceleration due to gravity = <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Weight of the body <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>64</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></math>
so the mass of the body <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6.4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi></math>, <math> <mrow> <msub> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mi>k</mi> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.4</mn></mrow> </math></p><p>
Net acceleration <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>Applied force -Kinetic friction</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>Mass of the body</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math> <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mi>s</mi> </msub> <mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><msub> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mi>k</mi> </msub> <mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow> <mi>m</mi> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msub> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mi>s</mi> </msub> <mo>&#8722;</mo><msub> <mi>&#956;</mi> <mi>k</mi> </msub> <mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0.6</mn><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mtext> </mtext><mi>g</mi></mrow> </math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q102. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An object of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>kg</mi></math> is connected to the lower end of a massless string of length <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> hanging from the ceiling. If a force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math> is applied horizontally at the mid-point of the string, the top half of the string makes an angle of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>45</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math> with the vertical, then the magnitude of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>75</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>90</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>70</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0629485.jpg" style="width: 236px; height: 177px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q103. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A wooden wedge of mass <math><mi>M</mi></math> and inclination angle <math><mi>α</mi></math> rests on a smooth floor. A block of mass <math><mi>m</mi></math> is kept on wedge. A force <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is applied on the wedge as shown in figure, such that a block remains stationary with respect to wedge. The magnitude of force <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">P</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-08-12_at_2.03.48_PM.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 100px; height: 57px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi> </mi><mi>α</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>(</mo><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cosec</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi> </mi><mi>α</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Since, <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>a</mi></math>

Now as in free body diagram of block,

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/9_que_CA.JPG" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 250px; height: 167px;" />

<math><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi> </mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></math> or <math><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q104. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Three blocks <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> of equal mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> are placed one over the other on a smooth horizontal ground as shown in the figure. Coefficient of friction between any two blocks is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn></math>. The maximum value of the mass of block <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> so that the blocks <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> move without slipping over each other is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-06-15_at_3.08.28_PM.jpeg" style="width: 200px; height: 140px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Blocks <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> both move due to friction. But less friction is available to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> as compared to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> because the normal reaction between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is less. Maximum friction between <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> can be

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo></math> Maximum acceleration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> can be

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

Further <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

Or <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

Or <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q105. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is placed at the top of a smooth wedge <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>. The wedge is rotated about an axis passing through <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> as shown in the figure. The minimum value of angular speed <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ω</mi></math> such that the block does not slip on the wedge is-

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image59-84f287a0-7eb5-4925-99a2-b76d20c2e5b5.jpg" style="height: 155px; width: 250px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>sin</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> <mi>l</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>sec</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <mrow><mo>(</mo>   <mrow>    <msqrt>     <mrow>      <mfrac>       <mrow>        <mi>g</mi><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext></mrow>       <mi>l</mi>      </mfrac>      </mrow>    </msqrt>    </mrow>  <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#x03B8;</mi></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <mrow><mo>(</mo>   <mrow>    <msqrt>     <mrow>      <mfrac>       <mi>g</mi>       <mrow>        <mi>l</mi><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mi>cos</mi><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mi>&#x03B8;</mi></mrow>      </mfrac>      </mrow>    </msqrt>    </mrow>  <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#x03B8;</mi></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <msqrt>   <mrow>    <mfrac>     <mrow>      <mi>g</mi><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mi>sin</mi><mi>&#x03B8;</mi></mrow>     <mi>l</mi>    </mfrac>    </mrow>  </msqrt>  <mi>a</mi></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <msqrt> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>sin</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> <mi>l</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> </msqrt> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mi>sec</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/74964_1.jpg" style="width: 200px; height: 111px;" />

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>m</mi><msup> <mi>ω</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>l</mi><msup> <mrow> <mi>cos</mi></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>sin</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>θ</mi></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>ω</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>sin</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>θ</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mi>l</mi><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mi>cos</mi></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mi>θ</mi></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </msqrt> <mo>=</mo><msqrt> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>g</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi>sin</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> <mi>l</mi> </mfrac> </mrow> </msqrt> <mi>sec</mi><mi>θ</mi></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q106. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

There is a trolley in which there is a fixed inclined surface on which a smooth block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> is placed. Two horizontal forces of magnitude <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are applied on the trolley as shown to keep the trolley at rest. The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo> </mo><mo>-</mo><mo> </mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is : (Assume there is no friction between the trolley and horizontal ground and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/32480.jpg" style="width: 250px; height: 113px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 5px; top: 38px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>24</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: -2px; top: -6px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

There is a trolley for which

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi>N</mi><mi>sin</mi><mn>37</mn><mo>°</mo><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/33.png" style="width: 464px; height: 205px;" />


<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>N</mi><mi>sin</mi><mn>37</mn><mo>°</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>. . . (1)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi><mn>=</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>37</mn><mo>°</mo></mrow></mfenced></math>. . . (2)

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>cos</mi><mn>37</mn><mo>°</mo><mi>sin</mi><mn>37</mn><mo>°</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>5</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>24</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

</details>

### Q107. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The minimum force required to move a body up an inclined plane is three times the minimum force required to prevent it from sliding down the plane. If the coefficient of friction between the body and the inclined plane is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the angle of the inclined plane is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>60</mn><mo>°</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>45</mn><mo>°</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>15</mn><mo>°</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>30</mn><mo>°</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Minimum force required to move a body up a rough inclined plane,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>mg</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></math>

Minimum force required to prevent the body from sliding down the rough inclined plane,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>mg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

According to the question,
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>mg</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#956;cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>mgsin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#956;mgcos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>30</mn><mo>&#176;</mo></math>

</details>

---

### Q108. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle slides down on a smooth incline of inclination <math><msup><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msup></math> , fixed in an elevator going up with an acceleration <math><mn>2</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo></math> The box of incline has width 4m. The time taken by the particle to reach the bottom will be

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/rlvesztbjetdqpncsutsclmhczfvlncxfnpurflwdfextvesce.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 163px; width: 130px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>s</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mi>s</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi>s</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi>s</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi>s</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/Screenshot_2020-09-03_at_9.13.12_PM.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain"/>
In the frame of elevator,

a = acceleration of the particle with respect to the elevator

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>m</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow></mrow><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi></math>

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi></math> The distance travelled by the particle from the top to the bottom,

<math><mi>d</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>30</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>o</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi></math>

Using <math><mi>s</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>u</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mi>a</mi><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> ,

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>×</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mn>6</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mi> </mi><mi>s</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q109. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The upper half of an inclined plane with inclination <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">ϕ</mi></math> is perfectly smooth while the lower half is rough. A body starting from rest at the top will again come to rest at the bottom if the coefficient of friction for the lower half is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mi>tan</mi><mi>ϕ</mi> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn></mn><mn>2</mn><mi>tan</mi><mi>ϕ</mi> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math>  <mrow>   <mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#981;</mi>  </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math>  <mrow>   <mn>2</mn><mi>sin</mi><mi>&#981;</mi>  </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow> <mn></mn><mn></mn><mn>2</mn><mi>tan</mi><mi>ϕ</mi> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For the smooth upper half, using <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>as</mi></math>, we get

           <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>sin&#981;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>

           <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mo>(</mo><mi>sin&#981;</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></msqrt></math>

For the lower half, using <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>as</mi></math>, we get

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>sin</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">&#981;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></msub><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>cos&#981;</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>sin&#981;</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>

or   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>sin&#981;</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></msub><mi>cos&#981;</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>sin&#981;</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></msub><mi>cos&#981;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>sin&#981;</mi></math>

or   <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>tan&#981;</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q110. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

At the instant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0,</mn></math> a force <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi></math> ( <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> is a constant) acts on a small body of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> resting on a smooth horizontal surface. The time, when the body leaves the surface is

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image5-1dda452d-cc3c-4aee-bf2d-607eb0fdcc3f.jpg" style="height: 106px; width: 200px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>sin</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></mrow><mi>k</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo> </mo><mi>sin</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo> </mo><mi>sin</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>F</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math>

The body leaves the surface when the normal reaction on it becomes zero.

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---
